```
Also cwd resets between bash calls; use absolute paths.
```

```
  private eocAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
    const T = TILE;
    if (!player) return;
    // 类字段默认 ai0=-1120 是史莱姆族约定初值;原版 ai[0] 从 0 起步(NewNPC 清零)。
    // 缺此归一时 ai0∉{0,1,2,3} 状态机无分支匹配 → 夜晚整段 P1/P2 全空转 =

    // 的入口级归一——2026-08-13 用户报"克眼呆在空中"

    const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);

    const flag2 = expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.12;

    // num4（:19917-19921）：连冲冲程，狂暴档减半

    if (flag3) num4 = 10;

    this.reflectsProjectiles = false;

    // ---- 白天/玩家死亡：向上漂离 + EncourageDespawn(10)（:20020-20025） ----
    // 原版在旋转段之后 return：状态机与 ai[2] 全部冻结，复活后从冻结处继续
    if (game.world.clock.isDay || player.dead) {
      this.vy -= 0.04;
      this.encourageDespawn(10);
      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
      if (dist > T * 120) this.dead = true;   // 漂远后兜底消失
      return;
    }

    if (this.ai0 === 0) {
      // ================= P1（ai[0]==0，:20026-20236） =================
      if (this.state === 0) {

            }
          }
        }
      } else if (this.state === 1) {
```

```
	{
		int num = 32;
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-19T05:45:00.873Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
King Slime vanilla complete (:43436-43753). Key facts:
- num (scale anim multiplier), num2=1 (gg: num3=2-1-lifeRatio → num2×=num3), flags.
- ai[3]==0 && life>0 → ai[3]=lifeMax (damage-dealt tracker for slime spawns).
- localAI[3]==0 first tick: ai0=-100, TargetClosest (no sound!).
- num4=3000: player dead or dist>3000 → TargetClosest; still → EncourageDespawn(10), face player, ai1=5 (teleport away), localAI[1]=maxTilesX*16, localAI[2]=maxTilesY*16 (teleport target = off-world → despawn teleport).
- Teleport trigger: !dead && timeLeft>10 && ai2>=300 && ai1<5 && vy==0 → ai2=0, ai0=0, ai1=5, find teleport spot (antiCheese if localAI0>=360 or dist>2000).
- No line of sight (CanHitLine) or top-vs-bottom >160: ai2++, localAI0++; else localAI0-- (min 0).
- timeLeft<10 && ai0/ai1 → reset to 0/0.
```

```
EoW chain logic captured (:51880-51914): segment self-checks:
- type 13/14/15: if both neighbors inactive → die (transfer damage to last attacker).
- head (13): if front (ai[0]) inactive → die.
- tail (15): if back (ai[1]) inactive → die.
- body (14): if back neighbor inactive/wrong aiStyle → Transform(13, ai[0]) (become head); if front inactive → Transform(15, 0, ai[1]) (become tail).

Continue with movement section (:51920-52100) — the head movement + non-head follow.
```

```
- Dig sound: EOWDiggin = custom trackable "eow_dig" (volume 1, pitchVar 0.02) — not a legacy int; types 13-15 play it while digging (soundDelay = clamp(dist/40,10,20)).
- Tail (:52650-52670): EoW head & segments fade in: dust 14 ×2 while alpha>0; alpha -= 42 when moved >2px. Wait — the return-gate: `if (type < 13 || type > 15 || (type != 13 && (type == 13 || Main.npc[(int)ai[1]].alpha >= 85)))` — for type 13 (head) → condition (type!=13 && ...) false → proceed; for 14/15: proceed only if npc[ai[1]].alpha < 85 (previous segment mostly visible). alpha starts 255 (SetDefaults). Head: no ai[1] (0) — head always proceeds. So fade-in chain gates visibility.
- Head rotation = atan2(vy,vx)+π/2.
- The despawn-when-no-corrupt-zone: EoW head dives down when not in corrupt zone; if deep & no player in corrupt → whole chain deactivates (no loot — active=false directly).
```

```
	private void CreateBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh()
	{
		int num = (int)(position.X + (float)(width / 2)) / 16;
		int num2 = (int)(position.Y + (float)(height / 2)) / 16;
		int num3 = width / 2 / 16 + 1;
		for (int i = num - num3; i <= num + num3; i++)
		{
			for (int j = num2 - num3; j <= num2 + num3; j++)
			{
				if ((i == num - num3 || i == num + num3 || j == num2 - num3 || j == num2 + num3) && !Main.tile[i, j].active())
				{
					Main.tile[i, j].type = (ushort)(WorldGen.crimson ? 347 : 140);
					Main.tile[i, j].active(active: true);
				}
				Main.tile[i, j].lava(lava: false);
				Main.tile[i, j].liquid = 0;
				if (Main.netMode == 2)
				{
					NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, i, j);
				}
				else
				{
					WorldGen.SquareTileFrame(i, j);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void DoDeathEvents_SummonDungeonSpirit(Player closestPlayer)
	{
		if (lifeMax > 100 && type != 288 && value > 0f && HasPlayerTarget && Main.hardMode && downedPlantBoss && Main.player[target].ZoneDungeon)
		{
			int range = 13;
			if (Main.expertMode)
			{
				range = 9;
			}
			if (closestPlayer.RollLuck(range) == 0 && Main.wallDungeon[Main.tile[(int)base.Center.X / 16, (int)base.Center.Y / 16].wall])
			{
				NewNPC(GetSpawnSource_NPCHurt(), (int)base.Center.X, (int)base.Center.Y, 288);
			}
		}
	}
```

```
export function skeletronHandAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const headId = e.ai1;
  const enemies = game.enemies() as unknown[];
  const head = enemies.find((n) => (n as Enemy).id === headId) as Enemy | undefined;
  e.facing = -(e.ai0 || 1);                        // spriteDirection = -(int)ai[0]（:22292）
  if (!head || head.vanilla?.aiStyle !== 11 || head.dead) {
    // :22294-22303 头失效 → ai[2] += 10,>50 或非服务器 → life=-1 即灭
    // （本仓恒单机 netMode!=2 → 首帧即死;>50 门仅专用服务器等待同步用）
    e.ai2 += 10;
    e.dead = true;
    return;
  }
  e.master = head; // 臂骨 IK 渲染用（Renderer.drawSkeletronArm）
  e.lai3 = head.ai3;                               // :22308 localAI[3] = 头 ai[3]
  const flag21 = e.lai3 === 1;                     // RedHatSkeletronAdjustmentsEnabled（type 36）
  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);
  if (flag21) {
    const b = baseOf(e);
    e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.3);        // :22310-22312 红帽伤害 ×1.3
  }
  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
  const headCx = head.x + head.w / 2;
  // 环绕锚点（rotation 基向,cs:22395-22404/22414-22423/22479-22488）
  const anchorRot = () => {
    const ax = headCx - 200 * e.ai0 - e.cx;
    const ay = head.y + 230 - e.cy;
    e.visAngle = Math.atan2(ay, ax) + 1.57;
  };
  if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
    // ---- 悬停态（cs:22313-22411）----
    if (head.ai1 === 3) e.encourageDespawn(10);    // :22314-22316 头离场 → 手劝退（非自灭!）
    if (head.ai1 !== 0 && !flag21) {
      // 头旋冲中：回侧位 ±120 / 头上 100（cs:22317-22358;Y 步 0.07 帽 ±6、X 步 0.1 帽 ±8,逆向 ×0.96）
      if (e.y > head.y - 100) {
        if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
        e.vy -= 0.07;
        if (e.vy > 6) e.vy = 6;
      } else if (e.y < head.y - 100) {
        if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
        e.vy += 0.07;
        if (e.vy < -6) e.vy = -6;
      }
      if (e.cx > headCx - 120 * e.ai0) {
        if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
        e.vx -= 0.1;
        if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
      }
      if (e.cx < headCx - 120 * e.ai0) {
        if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
        e.vx += 0.1;
        if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
      }
    } else {
      // 头悬浮：环绕位 ±200/+230 + ai[3] 计时（cs:22359-22407;红帽 +1/t、专家 +0.5/t
      // → 计满 300 提前;专家先跑一遍同参段再跑普通段 = 双倍步长）
      e.ai3 += 1;
      if (flag21) e.ai3 += 1;
      if (expert) e.ai3 += 0.5;
      if (e.ai3 >= 300) { e.ai2 += 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
      const passes = expert ? 2 : 1;
      for (let pass = 0; pass < passes; pass++) {
        if (e.y > head.y + 230) {
          if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
          e.vy -= 0.04;
          if (e.vy > 3) e.vy = 3;
        } else if (e.y < head.y + 230) {
          if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
          e.vy += 0.04;
          if (e.vy < -3) e.vy = -3;
        }
        if (e.cx > headCx - 200 * e.ai0) {
          if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
          e.vx -= 0.07;
          if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
        }
        if (e.cx < headCx - 200 * e.ai0) {
          if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
          e.vx += 0.07;
          if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
        }
      }
    }
    anchorRot();
  } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
    // ---- 蓄升态（cs:22412-22466）----
    anchorRot();
    e.vx *= 0.95;
    e.vy -= 0.1;
    if (flag21) {
      e.vy -= 0.09;
      if (e.vy < -15) e.vy = -15;
    } else if (expert) {
      e.vy -= 0.06;
      if (e.vy < -13) e.vy = -13;
    } else if (e.vy < -8) {
      e.vy = -8;
    }
    if (e.y < head.y - 200) {
      // 升到头上方 200 → 直冲（TargetClosest + 朝玩家 ×18/专家 21/红帽 24,cs:22448-22466）
      e.ai2 = 2;
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.max(0.01, Math.hypot(dx, dy));
      const spd = flag21 ? 24 : expert ? 21 : 18;
      e.vx = dx / d * spd;
      e.vy = dy / d * spd;
    }
  } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
    // ---- 直冲态（cs:22468-22476,纯弹道只查收尾门）----
    const dot = e.vx * (player.cx - e.cx) + e.vy * (player.cy - e.cy);
    if (e.y > player.y || dot <= 0
      || Math.hypot(player.x - e.x, player.y - e.y) > 2000 || e.vy < 0) {
      e.ai2 = 3;
    }
  } else if (e.ai2 === 4) {
    // ---- 侧浮态（cs:22478-22535）----
    anchorRot();
    e.vy *= 0.95;
    e.vx += 0.1 * -e.ai0;
    if (flag21) {
      e.vx += 0.1 * -e.ai0;
      if (e.vx < -15) e.vx = -15;
      else if (e.vx > 15) e.vx = 15;
    } else if (expert) {
      e.vx += 0.07 * -e.ai0;
      if (e.vx < -12) e.vx = -12;
      else if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12;
    } else if (e.vx < -8) {
      e.vx = -8;
    } else if (e.vx > 8) {
      e.vx = 8;
    }
    if (e.cx < headCx - 500 || e.cx > headCx + 500) {
      // 离心 500px → 回冲（×17/专家 22/红帽 25,cs:22514-22535）
      e.ai2 = 5;
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.max(0.01, Math.hypot(dx, dy));
      const spd = flag21 ? 25 : expert ? 22 : 17;
      e.vx = dx / d * spd;
      e.vy = dy / d * spd;
    }
  } else if (e.ai2 === 5) {
    // ---- 回冲态（cs:22537-22552）----
    const dot = e.vx * (player.cx - e.cx) + e.vy * (player.cy - e.cy);
    if ((e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) || (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx)
      || dot <= 0 || Math.hypot(player.x - e.x, player.y - e.y) > 2000) {
      e.ai2 = 0;
    }
  }
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}

function kingFindTeleportSpot(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player): { x: number; y: number } {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const px = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), py = Math.floor((player.y + player.h) / TILE);
  const cand: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
  for (const [rx, ry] of [[10, 10], [6, 2]] as const) {

    for (let x = px - rx; x <= px + rx; x++) {
      for (let y = py - ry; y <= py + ry; y++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.isSolid(x, y)) continue;
        if (st.isSolid(x, y - 1)) continue;                       // 顶上净空
        if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y - 1)] === 2) continue; // 岩浆
        if (!canHit(st, x * TILE + 8, (y - 1) * TILE + 8, 1, 1, player.x, player.y, player.w, player.h)) continue;
        // 外环 ±10/内环 ±7：|dx|>7 || |dy|>7 的环带才收（内 7×7 排除近身）
        if (rx === 10 && Math.abs(x - px) <= 7 && Math.abs(y - py) <= 7) continue;
        cand.push({ x, y });
      }
    }
    if (cand.length) break;
  }
  const pick = cand.length ? cand[Math.floor(Math.random() * cand.length)] : { x: px, y: py };
  return { x: pick.x * TILE + TILE / 2, y: (pick.y + 1) * TILE };   // Bottom 锚
}

export function kingSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
```
```
  // AI_015_KingSlime(NPC.cs:43436-43735)1:1 状态机:
  //  ai0 = 落地充能计数(负值回充,≥0 起跳;5/6 态复用为缩放计时)
  //  ai1 = 跳序 0→1→2→3→0(三小跳 + 一大跳)/5 缩身传送/6 淡入回归
  //  ai2 = 周期传送计数(无视线||高差>160 时累计)
  //  ai3 = 小史莱姆掉血水位(初值 lifeMax)
  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -100; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }  // :43454-43464
  if (e.ai3 === 0 && e.hp > 0) e.ai3 = e.maxHp;                          // :43450-43453
  let num = 1;          // 体型倍率(5/6 态缩放 1↔0.5)
  let flag2 = false;    // 传送态(5/6)=落地不充能
  let flag3 = false;    // 完全缩没(60t 起,dontTakeDamage)
  // 玩家死/远距 3000px(:43466-43490):TargetClosest 二次判定后仍失败 →
  // EncourageDespawn(10) + 背对 + ai[2]=0/ai[0]=0/ai[1]=5 缩身传送世界角
  const ksd = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) : Infinity;
  if (!player || player.dead || ksd > 3000) {
    e.encourageDespawn(10);
    if (player) e.facing = player.cx < e.cx ? 1 : -1;   // :43472-43479 转身背对
    if (e.ai1 !== 5 && e.ai1 !== 6) {
      e.ai2 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 5;
      kingTpDest.set(e, { x: game.world.w * TILE, y: game.world.h * TILE });  // :43486-43487
    }
  }
  // ---- 常规周期传送(:43491-43513):玩家活/timeLeft>10/计数≥300/非传送态/落地 →
  //  计数清零进 5;antiCheese(localAI[0]≥360 或距>2000px)直落玩家脚底,否则 FindTeleportSpot
  if (player && !player.dead && e.despawnTimer > 10 && e.ai2 >= 300 && e.ai1 < 5 && e.onGround) {
    e.ai2 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 5;
    let ac = kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0;
    const vec = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy);
    let antiCheese = false;
    if (ac >= 360 || vec > 2000) {
      if (ac >= 360) { ac = 360; kingAntiCheese.set(e, ac); }   // :43505-43508 钳 360
      antiCheese = true;
    }
    kingTpDest.set(e, antiCheese
      ? { x: player.cx, y: player.y + player.h }   // :43404-43409 FindTeleportSpot(antiCheese)
      : kingFindTeleportSpot(e, game, player));
  }
  // ---- 反挂机计数(:43514-43529,每 tick 无地面门):无视线||高差>160 →
  //  ai[2]+1 且 localAI[0]+1;否则 localAI[0]−1 钳 0(曾写成恒 +1=语义反,2026-08-19)。
  //  视线 = Center↔Center 连线(CanHitLine 0×0);高差 = Top.Y↔player.Bottom.Y ----
  if (player) {
    const los = canHit(game.world.store, e.cx, e.cy, 0, 0, player.cx, player.cy, 0, 0);
    const gap = Math.abs(e.y - (player.y + player.h));
    if (!los || gap > 160) {
      e.ai2 += 1;
      kingAntiCheese.set(e, (kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0) + 1);
    } else {
      kingAntiCheese.set(e, Math.max(0, (kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0) - 1));
    }
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 5) {
    // 缩身传送态(:43537-43573):num = clamp((60-ai0)/60)×0.5+0.5;60t 起 flag3
    // (隐没无敌);==60 Gore734 王冠;≥60 Bottom 锚落位 → 6 号;未隐没时每帧 10 蓝尘
    flag2 = true;
    e.ai0 += 1;
    num = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (60 - e.ai0) / 60)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    if (e.ai0 >= 60) flag3 = true;
    if (e.ai0 === 60) {
      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - e.h / 2, '#FFD060', 6, 2, { life: 40 }); // Gore 734 王冠(:43550)
    }
    if (e.ai0 >= 60) {
      const dest = kingTpDest.get(e) ?? { x: game.world.w * TILE, y: game.world.h * TILE };
      e.x = dest.x - e.w / 2;
      e.y = dest.y - e.h;                          // base.Bottom = (localAI[1], localAI[2])(:43554)
      e.ai1 = 6; e.ai0 = 0;
    }
    if (!flag3) game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#4E88FF', 10, 1.2, { life: 30 });  // :43564-43571
  } else if (e.ai1 === 6) {
    // 淡入回归态(:43574-43600):num = clamp(ai0/30)×0.5+0.5;30t 回战斗;到达尘 ×2 速
    flag2 = true;
    e.ai0 += 1;
    num = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, e.ai0 / 30)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    if (e.ai0 >= 30) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }
    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#4E88FF', 10, 2.4, { life: 30 });  // :43594-43599
  }
  e.dontTakeDamage = flag3;      // :43601 dontTakeDamage = (hide = flag3)
  e.squash = (1 - num) * 1.2;    // 体型倍率近似(缩放 0.5..1 ↔ squash 0.6..0)
  if (e.onGround) {
    // 落地(:43602-43663):vx 阻尼 0.8(<0.1 归零);非传送态充能起跳
    e.vx *= 0.8;
    if (e.vx > -0.1 && e.vx < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
    if (!flag2) {
      // :43611-43631 充能 +2/t + 血量分档(<80 +1/<60 +1/<40 +2/<20 +3/<10 +4 = +2..+13)
      e.ai0 += 2;
      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8) e.ai0 += 1;
      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.6) e.ai0 += 1;
      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) e.ai0 += 2;
      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.2) e.ai0 += 3;
      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.1) e.ai0 += 4;
      if (e.ai0 >= 0) {
        // 跳序(:43632-43656):ai1==3 → vy-13/vx+3.5dir/回充-200/回 0;
        // ai1==2 → vy-6/vx+4.5dir/回充-120/ai1+1;其余 → vy-8/vx+4dir/回充-120/ai1+1
        // (方向 = TargetClosest 的 direction,即玩家侧)
        const dir = player ? (player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1) : (e.facing || 1);
        e.facing = dir;
        if (e.ai1 === 3) { e.vy = -13; e.vx += 3.5 * dir; e.ai0 = -200; e.ai1 = 0; }
        else if (e.ai1 === 2) { e.vy = -6; e.vx += 4.5 * dir; e.ai0 = -120; e.ai1 += 1; }
        else { e.vy = -8; e.vx += 4 * dir; e.ai0 = -120; e.ai1 += 1; }
      }
      // :43658-43661 ai0 ≥ -30 = 起跳前蹲伏动画段(aiAction=1,squash 视觉)
    }
  } else if (player) {
    // 空中(:43664-43682):朝 direction 0.2/t 加速,限 |vx|<3(FTW 6);已反向时先 0.93 阻尼
    const num7 = game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 6 : 3;
    const dir = e.facing || 1;
    if ((dir === 1 && e.vx < num7) || (dir === -1 && e.vx > -num7)) {
      if ((dir === -1 && e.vx < 0.1) || (dir === 1 && e.vx > -0.1)) e.vx += 0.2 * dir;
      else e.vx *= 0.93;
    }
  }
  // 重力+地形 = 共享段(UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity + MoveWhileDry):0.3 重力/高空衰减/落速钳
  e.applyGravity(game);
  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
  if (e.hp <= 0) return;
  // ---- 掉血 5% 出小史莱姆(:43708-43734):水位 ai3 每越线补记;1-3 只;
  //  经典 1 号蓝史莱姆,专家 1/4 概率换 535(:43720-43723);体内随机点出、
  //  初速 Next(-15,16)*0.1 / Next(-30,1)*0.1、ai[0]=-1000*Next(3) ----
  const num10 = e.maxHp * 0.05;
  if (e.hp + num10 < e.ai3) {
    e.ai3 = e.hp;
    const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);
    const n = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);   // Next(1,4) = 1..3
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const s = spawnPart(game, e, expert && Math.floor(Math.random() * 4) === 0 ? 535 : 1);
      if (s) {
        s.x = e.x + Math.random() * Math.max(1, e.w - 32);
        s.y = e.y + Math.random() * Math.max(1, e.h - 32);
        s.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 31) - 15) * 0.1;
        s.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 31) - 30) * 0.1;
        s.ai0 = -1000 * Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
        s.ai1 = 0;
      }
    }
  }
}
```

```
export function brainOfCthulhuAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const st = game.world.store;
  // :32555-32567 TargetClosest 后曼哈顿 >6000 → active=false（记逃走）
  if (player && Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(player.cy - e.cy) > 6000) {
    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;
    return;
  }
  // 初次生成爬行者（:32540-32553）：GetBrainOfCthuluCreepersCount(:51330-51338)=20，
  // getGoodWorld（for the worthy 种子）= 40；出点 = 本体中心 ±(width/height) 随机，
  // 初速 = Next(-30,31)*0.1。爬行者锚定本体（原版 crimsonBoss 静态位 → master 引用）
  // 本仓 ai0 缺省 -1120 是史莱姆 hop 计数哨兵；原版 NPC.ai[0] 缺省 0（一阶段态）。
  // 放在 bInit 门之外：存档载入的实体同样需要归一（载入值 0/-1..-3 不受影响）
  if (e.ai0 === -1120) e.ai0 = 0;
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    for (let k = 0; k < (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 40 : 20); k++) {
      const c = spawnPart(game, e, 267);
      if (!c) continue;
      c.master = e;
      c.ai0 = 0; // 原版 ai[0] 缺省 0（环绕态）
      c.x = e.cx + (Math.floor(Math.random() * (2 * e.w)) - e.w) - c.w / 2;
      c.y = e.cy + (Math.floor(Math.random() * (2 * e.h)) - e.h) - c.h / 2;
      c.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.1;
      c.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.1;
    }
  }
  // 一阶段爬行者计数（:32726-32742）
  const creepers = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 267);

  if (e.ai0 < 0) {
    // ---- 二阶段（:32569-32699）----
    // :32575-32588 入场演出一次性（localAI[2] 门）：SoundID 3（Zombie_1）+
    //   血块 gore 392-395（position 左上角出、初速 Next(-30,31)*0.2 各自独立掷，
    //   NewGore 尾段另有 :1347-1348 扰动）+ 尘 5 号 ×20 + SoundID 15
    //   （ZombieMoan wav 未提取 → 略，登记）
    if (e.lai2 === 0) {
      e.lai2 = 1;
      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);
      for (let gi = 392; gi <= 395; gi++) {
        GorePiece.newGore(gi, e.x, e.y,
          (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.2,
          (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.2);
      }
      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#A03050', 20, 2);
      game.playSfxFiles(['Zombie_2'], 0.8, e.cx, e.cy);
    }
    // :32589 爬行者全灭后解除一阶段无敌（SetDefaults :12372 置位）
    e.dontTakeDamage = false;
    // :32590-32600 追踪：8 速逼近（(v*50+dir)/51）
    if (player) {
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      e.vx = (e.vx * 50 + (dx / d) * 8) / 51;
      e.vy = (e.vy * 50 + (dy / d) * 8) / 51;
    }
    if (e.ai0 === -1) {
      // :32603-32657 瞬移计时：localAI[1]+1，justHit 抵扣 Next(5)；
      //   达 60+Next(120)（单人无 netMode 附加段 :32611-32614）→ 选点 → ai[0]=-2
      e.lai1 += 1;
      if (e.justHit) e.lai1 -= Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
      const num859 = 60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 120);

        e.ai3 = 0;
        e.ai0 = -2;
        e.ai1 = spot.x; e.ai2 = spot.y;
      }
    } else if (e.ai0 === -2) {
      // :32660-32681 渐隐：v×0.9，ai[3]+=25/t（单人档），到 255 → 落点瞬移
      e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
      e.ai3 += 25;
      if (e.ai3 >= 255) {
        e.ai3 = 255;
        e.x = e.ai1 * TILE - e.w / 2;
        e.y = e.ai2 * TILE - e.h / 2;
        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_8'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // SoundID.Item8
        e.ai0 = -3;
      }
      e.spawnAlpha = e.ai3;
    } else if (e.ai0 === -3) {
      // :32682-32699 渐显：ai[3]-=25/t，≤0 回 -1
      e.ai3 -= 25;
      if (e.ai3 <= 0) {
        e.ai3 = 0;
        e.ai0 = -1;
      }
      e.spawnAlpha = e.ai3;
    }
  } else {
    // ---- 一阶段（:32701-32808）----
    // :32703-32723 缓追玩家：1 速（getGoodWorld ×3 :32708-32710），<1px 直达
    if (player) {
      let dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      let num870 = 1;
      if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num870 *= 3;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < num870) {
        e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
      } else {
        e.vx = (dx / d) * num870;
        e.vy = (dy / d) * num870;
      }
    }
    if (e.ai0 === 0) {
      // :32726-32742 爬行者清零 → 二阶段
      if (creepers.length === 0) {
        e.ai0 = -1;
        e.lai1 = 0;
        e.spawnAlpha = 0;
      }
      // :32743-32785 瞬移计时：120+Next(300) → 选点（12..40 格）→ ai[0]=1
      if (player) {
        e.lai1 += 1;
        if (e.lai1 >= 120 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 300)) {
          e.lai1 = 0;
          const spot = pickTeleportSpot(st, e, player, 12, 40, true);
          e.ai0 = 1;
          e.ai1 = spot.x; e.ai2 = spot.y;
        }
      }
    } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
      // :32788-32799 渐隐：+5/t 到 255 → 落点瞬移 → ai[0]=2
      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(255, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
      if (e.spawnAlpha >= 255) {
        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_8'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // SoundID.Item8
        e.x = e.ai1 * TILE - e.w / 2;
        e.y = e.ai2 * TILE - e.h / 2;
        e.ai0 = 2;
      }
    } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {
      // :32800-32808 渐显：-5/t 到 0 → ai[0]=0
      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 5);
      if (e.spawnAlpha <= 0) e.ai0 = 0;
    }
  }
  e.x += e.vx;
  e.y += e.vy;
  // :32810-32826 玩家死 || !玩家 ZoneCrimson（玩家离开猩红群系，SceneMetrics
  //   本帧快照 world.scene.zoneCrimson）→ 下坠逃离尾段（原版在状态机之后）：
  //   localAI[3] 钳 120，>60 后 vy+=(n-60)*0.25、ai[0]=2、alpha=10；恢复则计数递减。
  //   scene 为 null（本帧未扫/宿主无快照）视作"在猩红内"不逃离——原版 ZoneCrimson
  //   是玩家字段，扫描后才有意义，null 不等价 false
  const scene = game.world.scene;
  const playerGone = !player || player.dead || (scene != null && !scene.zoneCrimson);
  if (playerGone) {
    if (e.lai3 < 120) e.lai3 += 1;
    if (e.lai3 > 60) e.vy += (e.lai3 - 60) * 0.25;
    e.ai0 = 2;
    e.spawnAlpha = 10;
  } else if (e.lai3 > 0) {
    e.lai3 -= 1;
  }
}

/** 克脑瞬移选点（一阶段 :32748-32784 / 二阶段 :32620-32656 同构，参数不同）：
 *  以玩家所在格为原点，min..max 格随机偏移（各 50% 取负）；偏移向量与玩家速度
 *  同向（点积>0）时沿自身方向外推 16×speed px；一阶段收点条件 =
 *  101 次后无条件 或（非实心 且（76 次后 或 对玩家 CanHit）），二阶段 =
 *  101 次后无条件 或 非实心；越界点 = 非实心直接收（SolidTile 越界取空 tile，
 *  :32646/:32775 无 InWorld 门）。返回 tile 坐标。 */
function pickTeleportSpot(
  st: GameHooks['world']['store'], _e: Enemy, p: Player,
  min: number, max: number, losGate: boolean,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const pxSpeed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
  let tries = 0;
  let tx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE);
  for (;;) {
    tries++;
    let a = min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1));
    let b = min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1));
    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0) a *= -1;
    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 2) === 0) b *= -1;
    let vx = a * TILE, vy = b * TILE;
    // 玩家速度与偏移同向 → 外推（SafeNormalize 后 ×16×speed）
    if (p.vx * vx + p.vy * vy > 0) {
      const vl = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
      vx += (vx / vl) * 16 * pxSpeed;
      vy += (vy / vl) * 16 * pxSpeed;
    }
    tx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE) + Math.trunc(vx / TILE);
    ty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE) + Math.trunc(vy / TILE);
    // WorldGen.SolidTile 越界取默认空 tile → 非实心：越界点直接视"非实心+视线通"
    // 收下（NPC.cs:32646/:32775 收点条件无 InWorld 门，此前越界一律重掷是偏差）
    const inB = st.inBounds(tx, ty);
    const solid = inB && st.isSolid(tx, ty); // WorldGen.SolidTile
    const canSee = !losGate || !inB
      || canHit(st, tx * TILE, ty * TILE, 1, 1, p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h);
    if (tries > 100 || (!solid && (!losGate || tries > 75 || canSee))) break;
  }
  return { x: tx, y: ty };
}

/** 267 爬行者（NPC.cs:32828-32914 全核 1:1，SetDefaults :12388-12400）：
 *  ai[0]=0 环绕态：距主体 >90px 时 8 速逼近（(v*15+dir)/16）后 return；
 *  ≤90px 且 |v|<8 时两轴 ×1.05；直冲门 :32857 = `(expertMode && Next(100)==0) || Next(200)==0`
 *  （专家两掷串联：命中概率 1/100+(99/100)(1/200)≈1.5%）→ 8 速直冲玩家、ai[0]=1。
 *  ai[0]=1 扑咬态：专家追踪 :32872-32886——单位向量朝玩家 ×9（getGoodWorld ×12），
 *  (v*99+dir)/100（FTW (v*49+dir)/50）；经典档无追踪。
 *  距主体 >700px 回环绕；否则 !justHit 即 return（保持直飞），
 *  justHit 时按 knockBackResist 分流（=0 → 计数 ai[1] 超 5 回环绕，
 *  否则立即回环绕——267 原值 0.8 → 一击即回）。主体不在 → 消散（:32830-32835）。 */
```

```
export function creeperAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
  // 本仓 ai0 缺省 -1120（史莱姆哨兵）归一到原版缺省 0（环绕态）
  if (e.ai0 === -1120) e.ai0 = 0;
  const boss = e.master;
  // :32830-32835 crimsonBoss<0（主体不在）→ active=false
  if (!boss || boss.dead || boss.vanillaId !== 266) { e.dead = true; return; }
  // Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2762，getGoodWorld 抬档同样成立）
  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);
  const player = _player && !_player.dead ? _player : null;
  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
    // :32836-32856 环绕态：ai[1]=0；距主体 >90px 逼近
    e.ai1 = 0;
    const dx = boss.cx - e.cx, dy = boss.cy - e.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (d > 90) {
      e.vx = (e.vx * 15 + (dx / d) * 8) / 16;
      e.vy = (e.vy * 15 + (dy / d) * 8) / 16;
      e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
      return;
    }
    if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < 8) {
      e.vy *= 1.05;
      e.vx *= 1.05;
    }
    // :32857-32869 直冲门（TargetClosest 后 8 速直冲玩家、转扑咬态）：
    //   `netMode!=1 && ((expertMode && Next(100)==0) || Next(200)==0)`
    //   ★ 掷骰顺序照原版：专家先掷 Next(100)，未中再掷 Next(200)（短路求值）
    if (player && ((expert && Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) === 0) || Math.floor(Math.random() * 200) === 0)) {
      const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
      e.vx = (pdx / pd) * 8;
      e.vy = (pdy / pd) * 8;
      e.ai0 = 1;
    }
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  // ---- 扑咬态 ai[0]=1（:32871-32913）----
  // :32872-32886 专家追踪：朝玩家单位向量 ×9（FTW ×12），
  //   velocity = (v*99+dir)/100（FTW (v*49+dir)/50）——经典档直飞不追踪
  if (expert && player) {
    let tx = player.cx - e.cx, ty = player.cy - e.cy;
    const tl = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
    tx /= tl; ty /= tl;                       // vector101.Normalize()
    if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) {
      tx *= 12; ty *= 12;
      e.vx = (e.vx * 49 + tx) / 50;
      e.vy = (e.vy * 49 + ty) / 50;
    } else {
      tx *= 9; ty *= 9;
      e.vx = (e.vx * 99 + tx) / 100;
      e.vy = (e.vy * 99 + ty) / 100;
    }
  }
  // :32887-32894 距主体 >700px → 回环绕
  const bdx = boss.cx - e.cx, bdy = boss.cy - e.cy;
  if (Math.hypot(bdx, bdy) > 700) {
    e.ai0 = 0;
  } else {
    // :32897-32900 未受击保持直飞（经典档无追踪）
    if (!e.justHit) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
    // :32901-32912 knockBackResist==0（def 存承受比例：0=免疫）→ 计数 ai[1]>5 回环绕；
    //   否则一击即回（267 原值 0.8 走此支）
    if ((e.def.knockbackResist ?? 1) <= 0.01) {
      e.ai1 += 1;
      if (e.ai1 > 5) e.ai0 = 0;
    } else {
      e.ai0 = 0;
    }
  }
  e.x += e.vx;
  e.y += e.vy;
  void game;
}

// ================= 机械三王(NPC.cs AI_030-037,1.4.5.6 行号见各核注释) =================
// 经典模式参数 1:1;getGoodWorld(FTW)分支已全量实装(克脑/双子/毁灭者/爬行者);
// 专家/大师分支仅注释保留。史莱姆王 AI_015 的 FTW 分支(:43440/:43662)依赖的
// 基线行为(空中横移 num7=3、跳跃 num2 缩放)本仓未建模,无可挂载点,登记跳过。

/** 原版速度逼近(L26539-26670 雏形):朝目标速度单轴步进,符号翻转时双倍步进 */
export function accelTo(v: number, target: number, a: number): number {
  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a; }
  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a; }
  return v;
}
/** 朝 (tx,ty) 以速度 spd/加速度 acc 追赶(vanilla numXXX = spd/dist 归一后 accelTo) */
export function seek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, acc: number): void {
  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
}
```

```
export function skeletronBossAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const isGuardian = e.vanillaId === 68;
  // 初始化:生成双手 36(:21914-21931)
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    if (!isGuardian) {
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
        const h = spawnPart(game, e, 36);
        if (h) { h.ai0 = side; h.ai1 = e.id; h.ai3 = 150; h.bInit = true; }
      }
    }
  }
  // 白天 → ai1=2(守卫/离场语义);玩家死/超 2000px → 3
  // ★进入狂暴时冻结旋转方向(ai3):原版 rotation += direction*0.3 的 direction 是
  //  TargetClosest 的**持久**朝向字段(目标侧),非每帧 sign(vx)——曾用 facing
  //  (每帧 vx 符号)致旋冲过头贴脸时 spin 反复换号 = "只会两边摇动不旋转"
  if (game.world.clock.isDay && !isGuardian && e.ai1 !== 3) {
    if (e.ai1 !== 2) {
      // 旋冲方向用独立字段（原版读 direction 持久朝向）——★勿写 ai3：ai[3]==1 是
      // RedHatSkeletron 变体位（NpcDrops/Renderer/GorePiece 消费），曾挪用致普通
      // 骷髅王击杀时 ai3 常为 1 → 必掉全套 Chippy 时装（刷物品漏洞，2026-08-18 审查抓出）
      (e as unknown as { skeletronSpinDir?: number }).skeletronSpinDir = player && player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;
      // :21943-21947 转换瞬间 Roar 一声（SoundID 15 style0 = Roar_0,'roar' 键恒此轨）
      game.playSfx('roar', 1, e.cx, e.cy);
    }
    e.ai1 = 2;
  }
  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) {
    if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 2000) e.ai1 = 3;
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 2) {
    // 白天狂暴（:22247-22261）：damage/defense 均 9999、rotation+=0.3*direction、
    // 8 速恒直追——此前只改了伤害没改防御，且玩家死后 null 解引用
    e.def.damage = 9999;
    e.def.defense = 9999;
    e.spin += ((e as unknown as { skeletronSpinDir?: number }).skeletronSpinDir || 1) * 0.3;
    if (player) {
      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      e.vx = (dx / d) * 8; e.vy = (dy / d) * 8;
    }
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  if (e.ai1 === 3) {
    // 离场（:22262-22271）：vy+=0.1 下坠、vx 衰减、EncourageDespawn(50)——
    // 此前 y+=4 直落且永不 despawn，导致玩家死后 Boss 血条永久残留
    e.vy += 0.1;
    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;
    e.vx *= 0.95;
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    e.encourageDespawn(50);
    return;
  }
  if (!player) return;

  // ---- 专家分支一揽子(NPC.cs:21948-22004)----
  // num173 = 存活手数(type+1=36);defense += num173*25(:21958);
  // (num173<2 || 血<75%) 且悬浮态 → ai[2] 每 num175 tick 朝玩家发 270 追踪髅骨弹
  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);
  const num173 = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).filter(
    (n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === (e.vanillaId ?? 0) + 1).length;
  const b = baseOf(e);
  const defBase = b.def + (expert ? num173 * 25 : 0);   // :21958 专家按存活手数加防
  if (expert && (num173 < 2 || e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.75) && e.ai1 === 0) {
    // :21961-21969 num175=80(无手 40;FTW ×0.8);ai[2] 为悬浮计时(:22012)
    let num175 = 80;
    if (num173 === 0) num175 /= 2;
    if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num175 *= 0.8;
    if (e.ai2 % num175 === 0
      && canHit(game.world.store, e.cx, e.cy, 1, 1, player.x, player.y, player.w, player.h)) {
      // :21975-21999 num176=3(无手 +2);先 ±20px 抖动归一到 num176,再 ±0.5 速抖动
      // 归一 ×num176,最后 += 自身速度(骷髅王头本体常为零速,专家旋冲前近似为零)
      const num176 = 3 + (num173 === 0 ? 2 : 0);
      let sx = player.cx - e.cx + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20);
      let sy = player.cy - e.cy + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20);
      let sl = Math.hypot(sx, sy) || 1;
      sx = sx / sl * num176; sy = sy / sl * num176;
      let jx = sx + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 101) - 50) * 0.01;
      let jy = sy + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 101) - 50) * 0.01;
      sl = Math.hypot(jx, jy) || 1;
      jx = jx / sl * num176 + e.vx; jy = jy / sl * num176 + e.vy;
      shoot(game, e.cx + jx * 5, e.cy + jy * 5, jx, jy,
        getAttackDamageForProjectiles(17, 17, e.difficulty), 270);   // :21996-22000 timeLeft 300
    }
  }

  e.ai2 += 1;
  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
    // 悬浮段(:22046-22091):rotation = velocity.X/15(倾转);
    // 位移档 :22030-22055——经典 0.02/±2(Y) 0.05/±8(X),专家 0.03/±4 0.07/±9.5(:22034-22040),
    // FTW 追加 +0.01/+1/+0.05/+2(:22049-22055;RedHat 变体档不适用本核)
    e.spin = e.vx / 15;
    if (e.ai2 >= 800) {
      e.ai2 = 0;
      e.ai1 = 1;
      // 旋冲方向冻结 = 目标侧(TargetClosest direction 语义,见上 ai1=2 注)——独立字段勿占 ai3
      (e as unknown as { skeletronSpinDir?: number }).skeletronSpinDir = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1;
    }
    e.def.damage = b.dmg;               // :22007 悬浮段伤害基值复位
    e.def.defense = defBase;            // :21908/:21958(含专家手数加成)
    let nY = 0.02, cY = 2, nX = 0.05, cX = 8;
    if (expert) { nY = 0.03; cY = 4; nX = 0.07; cX = 9.5; }
    if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) { nY += 0.01; cY += 1; nX += 0.05; cX += 2; }
    if (e.y > player.y - 250) {
      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
      e.vy -= nY;
      if (e.vy > cY) e.vy = cY;
    } else {
      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
      e.vy += nY;
      if (e.vy < -cY) e.vy = -cY;
    }
    if (e.cx > player.cx) {
      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
      e.vx -= nX;
      if (e.vx > cX) e.vx = cX;
    } else {
      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
      e.vx += nX;
      if (e.vx < -cX) e.vx = -cX;
    }
  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
    // 旋冲段(:22105-22243):rot+=0.3*dir;速度 = 朝玩家单位向量 × num200 **每 tick 直设**
    // (:22240-22242,曾用 0.1 lerp 逼近 12 速=自造);num200 经典 1.5,专家 3.5 起步
    // + 距离阶梯(>150 ×1.05;>200..>600 每 100px ×1.1 共 10 级,:22179-22231)
    // + 按存活手数(0→×1.1 / 1→×1.05);FTW ×1.3(:22236-22239)。
    // 伤害 = GetAttackDamage_LerpBetweenFinalValues(defDamage, defDamage*1.3)
    // (:22174,与 ForProjectiles 同式 Remap——经典基值/专家 1.3×);
    // 防御 -10(:22153,在专家手数加成之后再减)
    if (e.ai2 === 2) game.playSfx('roar', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :22155-22157 ai[2]==2 入场吼(style0 满响,2026-08-18 对账补)
    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
    e.def.defense = Math.max(0, defBase - 10);
    e.def.damage = getAttackDamageForProjectiles(b.dmg, b.dmg * 1.3, e.difficulty);
    e.spin += ((e as unknown as { skeletronSpinDir?: number }).skeletronSpinDir || 1) * 0.3;
    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    let num200 = 1.5;
    if (expert) {
      num200 = 3.5;
      if (dist > 150) num200 *= 1.05;
      for (const d2 of [200, 250, 300, 350, 400, 450, 500, 550, 600]) if (dist > d2) num200 *= 1.1;
      if (num173 === 0) num200 *= 1.1;
      else if (num173 === 1) num200 *= 1.05;
    }
    if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num200 *= 1.3;
    e.vx = dx / dist * num200;
    e.vy = dy / dist * num200;
  }
  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}

/** Boss 伤害/防御基值缓存(状态机每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀) */
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
  return b;
};

let enemyCtor: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy'] | null = null;
/** Enemy 构造注入(Enemy.ts import 时回填,避免循环依赖) */
export function bindEnemyCtor(c: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy']) { enemyCtor = c; }

export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, src.cx, src.cy);
  if (!p) return null;
  return addEnemy(game, p);
}

/** 指定坐标生成原版怪（NPC.NewNPC 语义，Bottom 锚点由调用方换算）：
 *  spawnWOF（bossAI_wof）等无"母体"来源的生成点用 */
export function spawnVanillaEnemy(game: GameHooks, id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, x, y);
  if (!p) return null;
  return addEnemy(game, p);
}

/** Enemy.spawnWormChain 的代理出口（外部 AI 文件以 type-only 导入 Enemy，
 *  运行时引用会成环——绑定源同 spawnPart 的 enemyCtor）：建链并入 enemies 桶 */
export function spawnWormChainFor(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
  if (!enemyCtor) return [];
  const segs = enemyCtor.spawnWormChain(head, segCount);
  const em = (game as unknown as { entities?: { add?: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
  for (const s of segs) em?.add?.(s, 'enemies');
  return segs;
}
```

```
Also note: in master mode, Remap(difficulty, classic, expert, ...) with difficulty=3 (master) extrapolates beyond expert (difficulty float: classic 1? expert 2? master 3?) — Remap not clamped → master gets 2× expert value extrapolation. Hmm: vanilla: `damage = GetAttackDamage_LerpBetweenFinalValues(num37, num38); damage = GetAttackDamage_CappedAtMaster(damage);` — CappedAtMaster(normalDamage) = normalDamage × min(difficulty, Master) sample multiplier... wait that multiplies AGAIN by difficulty multiplier? Let me read CappedAtMaster: `float num = Math.Min(difficulty, Master); return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(num))`. Hmm — so the difficulty multiplier is applied by CappedAtMaster, and LerpBetweenFinalValues just lerps 23→18 by difficulty (where difficulty ∈ [1(classic), 2(expert), 3(master)] extrapolates to 13 in master). Then CappedAtMaster multiplies by EnemyDamageMultiplier sampled at min(difficulty, master)... That would double-apply expert multiplier (Remap already lerped by difficulty)... unless EnemyDamageMultiplier ≈ 1 at classic..master? In 1.4.5 the difficulty system changed: "difficulty" is a float (1=classic, 2=expert, 3=master?) and EnemyDamageMultiplier maps it (1→1.0? ...). Hmm — actually in 1.4.5.6, master = 3, and the multiplier table: classic 1.0, expert 1.0? Hmm no...

This is 1.4.5-specific (new GameDifficultyData). Our repo implements getAttackDamageCappedAtMaster with GDL.CLASSIC/EXPERT — the EoC comment says "经典 23 / 专家 36 / 大师 54". Hmm: Lerp(23,18) at difficulty 2 → 18? no... Let me not go down this rabbit hole; EoC P2 damage: vanilla Lerp(23, 18): classic=23, expert→18?? That gives LESS damage in expert (then expert multiplier from CappedAtMaster boosts it). Vanilla design: base contact 23 classic; expert base 18 but ×2 multiplier = 36; master: remap to 13 ×~4 = 52+.   private static readonly HIST_N = 10;
```
```
  private histX = new Float64Array(Enemy.HIST_N);
  private histY = new Float64Array(Enemy.HIST_N);

  /** fixedUpdate 尾部推入当前快照（位置=左上角，旋转=visAngle≈npc.rotation） */
  private pushHistory(): void {
    this.histHead = (this.histHead + 1) % Enemy.HIST_N;
    if (this.histCount < Enemy.HIST_N) this.histCount++;
    this.histX[this.histHead] = this.x;
    this.histY[this.histHead] = this.y;
    this.histRot[this.histHead] = this.visAngle;

  /** i tick 前的历史条数（拖尾最大可用深度） */
  historyDepth(): number { return this.histCount; }
  /** i tick 前的左上角 X（i 超出已填深度时取最旧条目） */
  histXAt(i: number): number {
    const d = Math.min(Math.max(0, i), this.histCount - 1);
    return this.histX[(this.histHead - d + Enemy.HIST_N * 2) % Enemy.HIST_N];
  }
  histYAt(i: number): number {
    const d = Math.min(Math.max(0, i), this.histCount - 1);

  }

  }

    if (this.vanillaId === 402) {

    if (!player) {

    const wormFlagType = this.vanillaId === 10 || this.vanillaId === 39 || this.vanillaId === 95

    const wormFlag = wormFlagType || (this.vanillaId === 513

    const playerAboveSurface = player.cy < game.world.groundLevel * TILE;
    if (player.dead || (wormFlag && playerAboveSurface)) {
      this.encourageDespawn(300);

    // 'roar' 键随机双轨 → 一半概率播成 boss 唤醒音,且 Roar_1 被 FILE_CASE_VOL 误

    if (this.vanillaId !== 621 && this.vanillaId !== 87 && this.vanillaId !== 117

      if (this.digSndDelay <= 0) {
        const dd = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) : 400;
        this.digSndDelay = Math.round(Math.min(20, Math.max(10, dd / 40)));
        game.playSfxWav?.('Roar_1', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      } else {

    let dx: number, dy: number;

    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    this.vx += (dx / d) * accel;
    this.vy += (dy / d) * accel;
    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
    this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    // 旋转（AI_006_Worms :52591 头/:51500 段）：贴图正面朝上 → rotation = atan2 + π/2。
    // 头朝目标（:52591 num49/50 = 朝向分量，等价速度角）；段用速度角（:51500）
    this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI * 0.5;
    // 蠕虫穿墙：直接位移（原版 noTileCollide）
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    // 段链跟随（原版 L52271-52308）：方向向量收缩维持 linkDist 间距——
    // shrink = (dist - linkDist)/dist；position += dxC*shrink（原版 num63/num64）
    for (let s = this.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
      const fx = s.wormFollow!;
      const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
      const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
      if (dist > 0.01) {
        const linkDist = s.w;               // 原版 num64 = width
        const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;

      if (dist > 0.01) s.visAngle = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI * 0.5;
```

```
    const st = game.world.store;
    const w = game.world;
    // 原版 target 即便玩家死亡仍指向其槽位（:51532 TargetClosest 后舵机照用其坐标）
    const tgt = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null;
    if (!tgt) return;
    // ---- 链维护（:51874-51914）----
    const linkAlive = (s: Enemy | null): boolean =>
      !!s && !s.dead && s.vanillaId !== null && s.vanillaId >= 13 && s.vanillaId <= 15;
    const prevOk = linkAlive(this.wormFollow), nextOk = linkAlive(this.wormNext);
    if (!prevOk && !nextOk) { this.die(game); return; }                   // :51876-51885 两邻全灭
    const kind0 = this.vanillaId ?? 0;
    if (kind0 === 13 && !nextOk) { this.die(game); return; }              // :51886-51895 头的下段灭
    if (kind0 === 15 && !prevOk) { this.die(game); return; }              // :51896-51905 尾的前段灭
    if (kind0 === 14) {
      if (!prevOk) {
        // :51906-51909 Transform(13, ai[0])：晋升新头（SetDefaults(13) 换头属性/贴图，
        // HP 按比例结转；三段 lifeMax 同为 150 → 原值保留）
        if (this.wormFollow) this.wormFollow.wormNext = null;
        this.wormFollow = null;
        this.transformTo(13, st, true, game);
      } else if (!nextOk) {
        // :51910-51913 Transform(15, 0, ai[1])：收成新尾
        if (this.wormNext) this.wormNext.wormFollow = null;
        this.wormNext = null;
        this.transformTo(15, st, true, game);
      }
    }
    // ---- 专家毒唾 666（:51468-51504）----
    const id = this.vanillaId ?? 0;
    if (w.isExpert && tgt) {
      if (id === 14 && (this.cy / TILE < w.groundLevel || w.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld)) {
        // :51470-51489 身段仅地表（中心格无墙）1/900 掷（FTW 1/450）
        const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)] === 0
          && Math.floor(Math.random() * (w.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 450 : 900)) === 0) {
          this.eowSpit(game, tgt);
        }
      } else if (id === 13) {
        // :51491-51503 头段 1/(90 + life/lifeMax×300) 掷
        const denom = 90 + Math.floor((this.hp / this.maxHp) * 300);
        if (Math.floor(Math.random() * denom) === 0) this.eowSpit(game, tgt);
      }
    }
    // ---- 身/尾段（ai[1]>0 → :52266-52321）：朝前段收缩维持链距（num64=width）----
    if (this.wormFollow) {
      const fx = this.wormFollow;
      const dxC = fx.cx - this.cx, dyC = fx.cy - this.cy;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
      if (dist > 0.01) {
        // :52300-52302 getGoodWorld 且 type∈13-15 → num64=62（段间距拉宽）；其余蠕虫族不覆写
        const vid = this.vanillaId ?? 0;
        const gap = (w.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld && vid >= 13 && vid <= 15) ? 62 : this.w;
        const shrink = (dist - gap) / dist;
        this.x += dxC * shrink;
        this.y += dyC * shrink;
        this.visAngle = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI / 2;
      }
      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;   // :52307 velocity = Vector2.Zero
      return;
    }
    // ---- 头段 ----
    // 玩家死（:51536-51543，EoW flag=false 不加速下钻）：离屏倒数消散
    if (tgt.dead) this.encourageDespawn(300);
    // 常数（:52091-52104）
    let maxSpd = 10, acc = 0.07;
    if (w.isExpert) { maxSpd = 12; acc = 0.15; }
    if (w.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) { maxSpd += 4; acc += 0.05; }
    // 埋地判定 flag3（:51968-51992）：AABB 外扩 1-2 格内实心块或液体>64
    let buried = false;
    const tx0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(this.x / TILE) - 1);
    const tx1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((this.x + this.w) / TILE) + 2);
    const ty0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(this.y / TILE) - 1);
    const ty1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE) + 2);
    for (let x = tx0; x <= tx1 && !buried; x++) {
      for (let y = ty0; y <= ty1; y++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        if (((st.flags[i] & 1) !== 0 && st.isSolid(x, y)) || st.liquid[i] > 64) {
          if (this.x + this.w > x * TILE && this.x < x * TILE + 16
            && this.y + this.h > y * TILE && this.y < y * TILE + 16) { buried = true; break; }
        }
      }
    }
    // :51993-52014 无玩家外扩矩形相交（>1000px）→ 视作埋地（远离时照走地下舵机）
    if (!buried && (tgt.dead
      || Math.abs(tgt.cx - this.cx) > 1000 + this.w
      || Math.abs(tgt.cy - this.cy) > 1000 + this.h)) buried = true;
    // 朝向分量（:52173-52259 tile 吸附差值）
    const dx = Math.floor(tgt.cx / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(this.cx / TILE) * TILE;
    const dy = Math.floor(tgt.cy / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(this.cy / TILE) * TILE;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (!buried) {
      // 空中（:52324-52371）：vy+=0.11 钳 num46；三档横漂（不主动转向）
      this.vy += 0.11;
      if (this.vy > maxSpd) this.vy = maxSpd;
      if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) < maxSpd * 0.4) {
        this.vx += this.vx < 0 ? -acc * 1.1 : acc * 1.1;
      } else if (this.vy === maxSpd) {
        if (this.vx < dx) this.vx += acc;
        else if (this.vx > dx) this.vx -= acc;
      } else if (this.vy > 4) {
        this.vx += this.vx < 0 ? acc * 0.9 : -acc * 0.9;
      }
    } else {
      // 掘地/咆哮音效（:52375-52395）：间隔 = num63/40 钳 10..20；
      // EoW 三段(13-15) = eow_dig 专属轨；【其余蠕虫族 = SoundID 15 Roar】——
      // 骨蛇 39-41/食世者等接近时的周期咆哮(用户报"骨龙接近有音效",2026-08-18)；
      // 排除表 621/87/117/454/412 不发声；EoW 另有 skyblock lowTiles 门(不涉)
      const isEow = (this.vanillaId ?? 0) >= 13 && (this.vanillaId ?? 0) <= 15;
      const digSndType = [621, 87, 117, 454, 412].includes(this.vanillaId ?? -1);
      if (!digSndType && this.digSndDelay <= 0) {
        this.digSndDelay = Math.round(Math.min(20, Math.max(10, dist / 40)));
        game.playSfx(isEow ? 'eowDig' : 'roar', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      } else {
        this.digSndDelay--;
      }
      if (Math.random() < 0.02) {
        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7A5A3C', 1, 0.9, { life: 28, damp: 0.96, grav: 0.06 });
      }
      // 目标速度（:52396-52401 归一到 num46 模长）
      let tvx = dx, tvy = dy;
      const d2 = Math.hypot(tvx, tvy) || 1;
      tvx = (tvx / d2) * maxSpd;
      tvy = (tvy / d2) * maxSpd;
      // 离开腐化下潜（:52402-52450）：目标 !ZoneCorrupt && !ZoneCrimson（或死）→ 直下潜。
      // 原版另有 flag7 多玩家扫描（任一存活玩家在腐化即不潜，:52417-52424）——
      // 单人等价于目标自身，联机 Boss 权威在房主，略（登记）
      const scene = w.scene;
      const zoneOk = scene == null ? true : (scene.zoneCorrupt || scene.zoneCrimson);
      if (tgt.dead || !zoneOk) {
        if (this.cy / TILE > (w.rockLevel + w.h) / 2) {
          // :52427-52446 深度达标：头 + 后继链整体清除（active=false，无掉落无记账）
          this.bossFled = true;
          for (let s: Enemy | null = this; s; s = s.wormNext) s.dead = true;
          return;
        }
        tvx = 0; tvy = maxSpd;   // :52447-52449
      }
      // 全向舵（:52511-52597，与毁灭者地下核同构）
      const sameX = (this.vx > 0 && tvx > 0) || (this.vx < 0 && tvx < 0);
      const sameY = (this.vy > 0 && tvy > 0) || (this.vy < 0 && tvy < 0);
      // :52513 首分支 = 四条件【跨轴 OR】(sameX || sameY)——单轴同向即走温和支；
      // 曾抄成 &&（毁灭者 :50481/:50500 才是 &&/|| 双块结构，勿混）
      if (sameX || sameY) {
        if (this.vx < tvx) this.vx += acc; else if (this.vx > tvx) this.vx -= acc;
        if (this.vy < tvy) this.vy += acc; else if (this.vy > tvy) this.vy -= acc;
        if (Math.abs(tvy) < maxSpd * 0.2 && ((this.vx > 0 && tvx < 0) || (this.vx < 0 && tvx > 0))) {
          this.vy += this.vy > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2;
        }
        if (Math.abs(tvx) < maxSpd * 0.2 && ((this.vy > 0 && tvy < 0) || (this.vy < 0 && tvy > 0))) {
          this.vx += this.vx > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2;
        }
      } else if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
        if (this.vx < tvx) this.vx += acc * 1.1; else if (this.vx > tvx) this.vx -= acc * 1.1;
        if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) < maxSpd * 0.5) {
          this.vy += this.vy > 0 ? acc : -acc;
        }
      } else {
        if (this.vy < tvy) this.vy += acc * 1.1; else if (this.vy > tvy) this.vy -= acc * 1.1;
        if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) < maxSpd * 0.5) {
          this.vx += this.vx > 0 ? acc : -acc;
        }
      }
    }

    this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }
```

```
        const netId = this.vanillaNetId !== 0 ? this.vanillaNetId : this.vanillaId;
        const ctx: NpcDropCtx = {
          rng,
          netId,
          type: this.vanillaId,
          isExpert: w.isExpert, isMaster: w.isMaster,
          hardMode: !!w.flags.hardMode, crimson: !!w.crimson,
          flags: w.flags,
          x: this.cx, y: this.cy,
          worldSurfaceTile: w.groundLevel, rockLayerTile: w.rockLevel,
          underworldTileY: w.lavaLine || w.h - 200,
          maxTilesX: w.w, maxTilesY: w.h,
          lifeMax: this.maxHp, friendly: false, boss: this.killBossFlag,
          damage: this.def.damage ?? 0,
          // value：ScaleStats 后的缩放值（def.value，NPC.cs:18110 EnemyMoneyDropMultiplier）；
          // 雕像产怪 value=0（Wiring.cs:2479）——雕像归零优先于难度缩放。
          // ★负 netID 变体走 npcValueOf(netId) 原值：原版 SetDefaultsFromNetId（NPC.cs:7633+）
          //   先 SetDefaults_ForNetId（含 ScaleStats）再硬覆写 damage/life/value 常数，
          //   覆写后【不再缩放】→ 变体 value 是未缩放原值，不得用基底缩放值顶掉
          value: this.spawnedFromStatue ? 0
            : (netId === this.vanillaId ? (this.def.value ?? npcValueOf(netId)) : npcValueOf(netId)),
          statueSpawned: this.spawnedFromStatue,
          hp: p.hp, hpMax: p.maxHp, mana: p.mana, manaMax: p.maxMana,
          luck: p.luck,
          moonEvent: game.moonEventCtx?.() ?? undefined,
          ai3: this.ai3, // NPC ai[3]：RedHatSkeletron / 光之女皇暴怒掉落条件
          ai1: this.ai1, // NPC ai[1]：史莱姆体内物品 id（SlimeBodyItemDropRule 数据源，
          //  Enemy.slimeRollContainedItem 首帧掷定；-1=掷过为空，0=未掷）
          // NPC.HasPlayerTarget（NPC.cs:6603）：TryTrackingTarget 无距离门，
          // 单人 = 玩家存活非 ghost——悠悠球五条件（Conditions.cs:1136-1244）数据源
          hasPlayerTarget: !!p && !p.dead,
          anyNpcOfType: (t) => game.enemies().some((o) => {
            const e2 = o as Enemy;
            return e2 !== this && !e2.dead && e2.vanillaId === t;
          }), // NPC.AnyNPCs：MissingTwin（先死的那只眼不掉）
          extras: game.dropContext?.(),
        };
        const unmapped = (id: number) => {
          if (!UNMAPPED_WARNED.has(id)) { UNMAPPED_WARNED.add(id); console.warn(`[NpcDrops] 物品 ${id} 无 key 映射，跳过`); }
        };
        const spawn = (id: number, n: number): ItemDrop | null => {
          const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id];
          if (!key) { unmapped(id); return null; }
          const v = dropVelocity(rng, id);
          const drop = game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, key, n, v.vx, v.vy);
          // 心(58)/星(184) 标记为 pickup：触碰回血/回蓝，不进背包
          if (drop) {
            if (id === 58) drop.pickup = 'heart';
            else if (id === 184) drop.pickup = 'star';
          }
          return drop;
        };
        const res = resolveDrops(ctx);
        for (const d of res.drops) {
          const drop = spawn(d.id, d.n);
          // 凝胶染色（CommonCode.ModifyItemDropFromNPC）：gel(item 23) 由 type 1 掉落且
          // netID∉{-1,-2,-5,-6} → item.color = npc.color（当前渐变色）
          if (drop && d.id === 23 && this.vanillaId === 1
            && this.vanillaNetId !== -1 && this.vanillaNetId !== -2
            && this.vanillaNetId !== -5 && this.vanillaNetId !== -6 && this.colorRGBA) {
            drop.color = [...this.colorRGBA];
          }
        }
        // 地牢之魂（DoDeathEvents_SummonDungeonSpirit，NPC.cs:79863-79878）：世纪之花
        // 已击败 + 目标玩家身处地牢，击杀 lifeMax>100、value>0、非 288 的敌怪 →
        // closestPlayer.RollLuck(13/专家9)==0（★luck 改掷，2026-08-15 前误为均匀掷）
        // 且【本 NPC 中心格墙为地牢墙】（Main.wallDungeon 7/8/9/94-99——原版双门，
        // 此前缺墙门=误在砖外刷魂）→ 原地刷 288
        if (this.vanillaId !== 288 && ctx.lifeMax > 100 && ctx.value > 0 && ctx.hasPlayerTarget
          && ctx.hardMode && ctx.flags['downed_262']
          && (game as unknown as { scene?: { zoneDungeon?: boolean } }).scene?.zoneDungeon
          && rollLuck(ctx.luck ?? 0, ctx.isExpert ? 9 : 13, () => rng.next()) === 0) {
          const st2 = game.world.store;
          const wx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), wy = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
          if (st2.inBounds(wx, wy) && DUNGEON_WALL_IDS.has(st2.wall[st2.idx(wx, wy)])) {
            spawnPart(game, this, 288);
          }
        }
        // 双子 BeforeLoot（NPC.cs DoDeathEvents_BeforeLoot :79761）：另一只眼还活着时
        // value=0 + boss=false——钱与 Boss 药水/心只给后死的那只（先死的只出规则掉落）
        const twinSuppressed = (this.vanillaId === 125 || this.vanillaId === 126)
          && game.enemies().some((o) => {
            const e = o as Enemy;
            return e !== this && !e.dead
              && e.vanillaId === (this.vanillaId === 125 ? 126 : 125);
          });
        // 钱币（规则未清零 value 时走 NPCLoot_DropMoney 管线）
        const value = res.zeroValue || twinSuppressed ? 0 : ctx.value;
        if (value > 0) for (const c of rollCoins(value, ctx, !!ctx.extras?.bloodMoon, this.midasT > 0)) spawn(c.id, c.n);
        // 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana）
        for (const h of rollHeartsAndStars(ctx)) spawn(h.id, h.n);
        // Boss 药水+心（DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts，boss 门 :80307）——
        // EoW 中间段 killBossFlag=false 不出（仅最后一段）
        if (this.killBossFlag && !twinSuppressed) for (const b of rollBossPotionsAndHearts(ctx)) spawn(b.id, b.n);
        }
      } else {
        for (const d of this.def.drops) {
          if (rng.next() < d.chance) {
            const n = rng.int(d.min, d.max);
            if (n > 0) game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, d.item, n, rng.range(-1.5, 1.5), -2);
          }
        }
      }
      game.onEnemyKilled(this.key, this);
      // 死亡声 = 本体 DeathSound（NPC.Strike :48316 全 NPC 同链，Boss 亦然——
      // 血肉墙 NPCDeath10 / 机械族 NPCDeath14 / 克脑 NPCDeath11 / 猪鲨 NPCDeath20 /
      // 光皇 65 / 史后 64 / 异教徒 59 / 月总头手 62 / Betsy DD2 / 鹿角怪专属）。
      // HitEffect 里唯一额外 Boss 咆哮 = EoC（NPC.cs:88205-88210：gore 循环体内
      // PlaySound(15)，×2 次迭代 → 连播两声）；月总核心 398 DeathSound=null 无声。
      // （旧"Boss 一律咆哮吞掉 DeathSound"2026-08-14 对账修正）
      if (this.vanillaId === 4) {
        game.playSfx('roar', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
        game.playSfx('roar', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
      }
      if (this.vanillaId !== 398) {
        if (this.def.killedSound.length) game.playSfxFiles?.(this.def.killedSound, 1, this.cx, this.cy);
        // 空 = 显式无声(原版 DeathSound=null,246 头:血尽本应转 249,标准死零死音)——
        // 旧 'killed' 合成 fallback 无可达场景(fromVanilla 恒非空除 null 路径),撤
      }
      // 原版死亡 gore 碎块（NPC.HitEffect life<=0 段全表，NPC.cs:82421-:90873 →
      // tools/extract-gore.mjs → src/data/vanilla-gore.json）：查表炸 GorePiece
      // （位置/速度/ai 门/gates 守卫/post 后处理 1:1，Gore.NewGore 随机扰动在
      // GorePiece.newGore 内）。spriteDirection = 本仓 facing（同符号约定，表内
      // `spriteDirection != -1` 类镜像分支门用）。无表项 = 原版只出尘不出块
      // （史莱姆族等）→ 只走下方彩色粒子近似
      spawnNpcDeathGore(this, game, Math.sign(kbx) || 1, this.facing);
      // 死亡碎裂：与主角同款物理粒子（喷溅/落地反弹/停驻淡出），颜色按怪物类型
      // （兼作原版 HitEffect 的血尘近似，与 gore 碎块叠加）
      const [c1, c2, c3] = this.def.gore;
      const big = this.killBossFlag || this.w > 40;
```

```
// ============ 原版 FindFrame 分族帧引擎（1.4.5.6 Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs:67295+） ============

/** TownNPCs 档案贴图条帧数取值口径（2026-08-13 两轮修正教训）：
 *  帧高逐表可变（人形族 54-64px、城镇史莱姆 670/678-684 仅 34-44px）→ 高/56 反推
 *  对史莱姆必错；唯一权威 = vanilla-npcs.json frames（=Main.npcFrameCount）。
 *  atlas.vnpcMeta 只覆盖 vanilla.json npcs 的 20 条特殊条目（46/49/67/74/299…,
 *  城镇 NPC 均不在其中）→ 必须回退 json frames,勿再 ??1（整条贴图走路根因） */
export function townSheetFrameCount(npcId: number): number {
  return VANILLA_NPCS[String(npcId)]?.frames ?? 1;
}

/** 玩家本体随坐骑机身倾斜的绘制变换（LegacyPlayerRenderer → PlayerDrawLayers 1:1）：
 *  原版 DrawPlayerFull 把 player.fullRotation/fullRotationOrigin 整组传入
 *  PlayerDrawSet（LegacyPlayerRenderer.cs:481/:307/:312），DrawPlayer_TransformDrawData
 *  （PlayerDrawLayers.cs:4199-4230）对 DrawDataCache【全部层】绕
 *  drawinfo.Position+rotationOrigin 旋转并把 fullRotation 加进每层自转——即手持物/
 *  翅膀/纸娃娃各层与机身贴图作为刚体同转（例外 ignorePlayerRotation：钻头激光
 *  Mount.cs:6061；Safeman 日环 PlayerDrawLayers.cs:4179）。
 *  锚点（fullRotationOrigin 来源，均为 mounted 盒坐标系——盒高 42+heightBoost 底锚）：
 *   - UFO 7 / 钻头 8：Mount.SetMount（Mount.cs:6268-6271）= (width/2, height/2)
 *     → pivotY = 玩家盒底 − (42+boost)/2
 *   - 巫婆扫帚 23：Hover 每帧（Mount.cs:3520）= (width/2, height) → 玩家盒底心
 *  矿车族不适用（fullRotation 走 cart.rotation 的 TrackRotation 独立链，已另行消费）。
 *  返回 null = 不旋转（角度 0 / 非倾斜族）。 */
export interface PlayerMountRotation { angle: number; pivotX: number; pivotY: number }
export function playerMountRotation(
  type: number, fullRotation: number,
  box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, heightBoost: number,
): PlayerMountRotation | null {
  if (fullRotation === 0 || (type !== 7 && type !== 8 && type !== 23)) return null;
  const mh = 42 + (heightBoost || 0);   // mounted 盒高（本仓玩家盒恒 42，底锚换算）
  return {
    angle: fullRotation,
    pivotX: box.x + box.w / 2,
    pivotY: type === 23 ? box.y + box.h : box.y + box.h - mh / 2,
  };
}

/** 上变换的 canvas 施加（TransformDrawData 位旋矩阵的等价写法，与矿车旋转同式） */
function applyPlayerMountRotation(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, r: PlayerMountRotation): void {
  ctx.translate(r.pivotX, r.pivotY);
  ctx.rotate(r.angle);
  ctx.translate(-r.pivotX, -r.pivotY);
}

// 僵尸族 case 3（L77026）：腾空/逆向→帧2；站定→帧0；行走 counter+=|vx| 按 8/16/24/32 → 0,1,2,1 往复
const ZOMBIE_FRAME_TYPES = new Set([3, 52, 53, 132, 161, 186, 187, 188, 189, 200, 223, 251, 254, 255, 319, 320, 321, 331, 332, 342, 536, 590, 691]);

const BAT_SKIP_LAST = new Set([49, 51, 60, 634]);
// 法师族三态帧（FindFrame case 29/32/45/172/281-286/693，cs:77721-77743）——
// 非循环状态机：帧0 待机 / 帧1 蓄力(ai[1]>0 且 vy==0) / 帧2 腾空(vy≠0)；
// 24 火妖 / 533 沙漠幽魂另有专属 case（下方分支）
const CASTER_STATE_FRAME_TYPES = new Set([29, 32, 45, 172, 281, 282, 283, 284, 285, 286, 693]);
// 旋转族 NPC（原版 npc.rotation 驱动绘制朝向；FindFrame 不做朝向翻转）：
// 35/68=骷髅王头/守卫、113-115=血肉墙/之眼/饥饿者、116=饥饿者 II(FindFrame case 116
//   rotation=atan2(vy,vx)，vx>0 再 FlipHorizontally——drawEnemy 独占分支自带翻转，
//   登记进 rotationDriven 以禁用通用 facing 镜像)、125/126=双子、127-131=Prime 头+四部件、
// 134-136=毁灭者链、261-265=世花族(孢子/本体/钩蔓/触须)、370=猪鲨、396/397=月总头/手、657=史莱姆皇后(飞行倾斜)
const ROTATION_NPC = new Set([35, 68, 113, 114, 115, 116, 125, 126, 127, 128, 129, 130, 131, 134, 135, 136, 246, 247, 248, 249, 261, 262, 263, 264, 265, 370, 396, 397, 657]);

// Main.wofDrawFrameIndex（Main.cs:820 静态；同屏只有一只血肉墙，模块级等价）。
// 原版在 DrawWOFBody 内自增（:37861-37863），bossAI_wof 未维护 → 渲染侧持有。
// 18 步循环、/6 换帧 = 3 帧各 6 步；gamePaused 冻结语义本仓无暂停态，恒推进（差异登记）
let wofDrawFrameIndex = 0;

// ---- 状态化 FindFrame case（WeakMap 持 counter/frame,每调用推进一次 ≈ 每 tick）----
/** 鸮族 611/689 站定转头待机（NPC.cs:74383-74429 1:1,替代顺序轮播近似）:
 *  counter 每 tick +1（band 边界 1/80 掷停顿）；(counter+1)%40==39 → 重掷 band
 *  = 40×rand(3)。band0=[0,1,2,1] band1=[0,3,4,3] band2=[0,5,6,7]（各帧 10t） */
const owlIdleStates = new WeakMap<Enemy, { counter: number }>();
function owlIdleFrame(e: Enemy): number {
  const s = owlIdleStates.get(e) ?? { counter: 0 };
  owlIdleStates.set(e, s);
  if (Math.trunc(s.counter) % 40 !== 0 || Math.random() * 80 < 1) s.counter += 1;
  if ((s.counter + 1) % 40 === 39) s.counter = 40 * Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
  const num113 = Math.floor((s.counter % 40) / 10);   // C# 整除((int)counter%40/10)——曾浮点除致帧号 2.5/7.3
  const num114 = Math.trunc(s.counter / 40);
  if (num114 === 0) return num113 === 3 ? 1 : num113;
  if (num114 === 1) return num113 === 0 ? 0 : 2 + (num113 === 3 ? 1 : num113);
  return num113 === 0 ? 0 : 4 + num113;
}

/** 珍稀宝箱怪 473-476 状态机（NPC.cs:71831-72000 1:1,替代周期穿插近似）:
 *  ai0 0/7 伪装帧0；1 警戒按 ai1/6 分段 1-6；8 悬浮 3+(counter/6) 钳 3-6
 *  （counter 24 回卷）；2/6 地面 = 低帧抬 12→<10 每 8t +1 至 10（counter=12）、
 *  ≥10 后 6t 眨 12/11/10/11（23 回卷），腾空=13；3 每 6t 从 >7 递减回落；
 *  4/5=13。原版 ai[0]==4.1 特殊段（三段跳劈帧序）本仓 ai0 为整数不产生,备案 */
const mimicStates = new WeakMap<Enemy, { counter: number; frame: number }>();
function mimicFrame(e: Enemy, frames: number): number {
  const s = mimicStates.get(e) ?? { counter: 0, frame: 0 };
  mimicStates.set(e, s);
  const ai0 = e.ai0;
  if (ai0 === 0 || ai0 === 7) { s.counter = 0; s.frame = 0; return 0; }
  if (ai0 === 1) {
    s.counter = 0;
    s.frame = e.ai1 < 6 ? 1 : e.ai1 < 12 ? 2 : e.ai1 < 18 ? 3 : e.ai1 < 24 ? 4 : e.ai1 < 30 ? 5 : 6;
    return s.frame;
  }
  if (ai0 === 8) {
    s.counter += 1;
    if (s.counter >= 24) s.counter = 0;
    s.frame = Math.min(6, Math.max(3, 3 + Math.trunc(s.counter / 6)));
    return s.frame;
  }
  if (ai0 === 3) {
    s.counter += 1;
    if (s.counter > 6) s.counter = 0;
    if (s.frame > 7) s.frame -= 1;
    return s.frame;
  }
  if (ai0 === 4 || ai0 === 5) { s.counter = 0; s.frame = 13; return Math.min(13, frames - 1); }
  // ai0 2/6
  if (e.vy === 0) {
    s.counter += 1;
    if (s.frame < 7) s.frame = 12;
    if (s.frame < 10) {
      if (s.counter > 8) {
        s.frame += 1;
        s.counter = 0;
        if (s.frame === 10) s.counter = 12;
      }
    } else if (s.counter < 6) s.frame = 12;
    else if (s.counter < 12) s.frame = 11;
    else if (s.counter < 18) s.frame = 10;
    else {
      s.frame = 11;
      if (s.counter >= 23) s.counter = 0;
    }
  } else {
    s.counter = 0;
    s.frame = 13;
  }
  return Math.min(frames - 1, s.frame);
}

/** 按原版 FindFrame 分族规则算当前帧 index（导出供 DebugReport tiles.focus.frameEngine 复算） */
export function vanillaFrameIdx(e: Enemy, frames: number): number {
  // AI 侧算定的帧号直读（694 水书宝箱怪态机帧——态切换清零的 frameCounter
  // 无法从全局 t 推出，cursedSkullAI 的 mimic694Frame 维护）
  if (e.customFrameIdx != null) return Math.min(frames - 1, e.customFrameIdx);
  const id = e.vanillaId ?? 0;
  const ai = e.vanilla?.aiStyle ?? 0;
  const t = e.animT; // tick 计数（≈原版 frameCounter 驱动源）
  const walking = Math.abs(e.vx) > 0.05;
  // 僵尸族（L77049-77085）：行走 0,1,2,1 按 |vx| 累加；腾空=2；站定=0
  if (ZOMBIE_FRAME_TYPES.has(id)) {
    if (!e.onGround) return Math.min(2, frames - 1);
    if (!walking) return 0;
    const phase = (e.walkCycleT + Math.abs(e.vx) * 8) % 32; // 每 tick +|vx|，32 一循环
    return phase < 8 ? 0 : phase < 16 ? 1 : phase < 24 ? 2 : 1;
  }
```

```
    const mirrored = e.facing > 0;   // 同通用路径朝向镜像（贴图默认朝左）
    // 四镜像幻影（Main.cs:24802-24841）
    const pl = this.mainPlayer;
    const ftw = !!world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld;
    if (pl && e.hp < e.maxHp && (world.isExpert || ftw)) {
      let k = 1 - e.hp / e.maxHp;
      if (world.isExpert) k *= 2;
      k *= k;
      if (ftw) k = 1;
      k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, k));
      const dx = Math.abs(e.cx - pl.cx), dy = Math.abs(e.cy - pl.cy);
      for (let n = 0; n < 4; n++) {
        const sx = n === 0 || n === 2 ? 1 : -1;   // :24822-24829
        const sy = n === 0 || n === 1 ? 1 : -1;   // :24831-24838
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(pl.cx + sx * dx, pl.cy + sy * dy + yOff);
        ctx.scale(scale, scale);
        if (mirrored) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, opacity * k));
        ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, -r.sw / 2, -r.sh / 2, r.sw, r.sh);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    // 本体（同通用路径中心锚 + 朝向镜像 + Y 偏移）
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(e.cx, e.cy + yOff);
    ctx.scale(scale, scale);
    if (mirrored) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
    ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;
    ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, -r.sw / 2, -r.sh / 2, r.sw, r.sh);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

    const glowB = this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Glow_134.png');
    const glowH = this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Glow_135.png');
    if (!head || !segA || !segB) return;
    const scale = (e.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * e.vanillaScale;
    const opacity = 1 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0)) / 255; // NPC.Opacity
    // Utils.Remap（:23860-23861 渐隐曲线）
    const remap = (x: number, a: number, b: number, c: number, d: number): number =>
      x <= a ? c : x >= b ? d : c + ((d - c) * (x - a)) / (b - a);
```

```
export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300, arrowOpts?: Partial<import('./Arrow').ArrowOpts>): void {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1, hostile: true, ...arrowOpts }), 'projectiles');
}

/** NPC.SpawnMechQueen 合体召唤链(NPC.cs:19739-19748,Game.summonMechdusa 调用)：
 *  queen(127) 落位 → markMechQueen(=SpawnBoss :81472 写 ai[3]=mechQueen 非零标记)
 *  → 以 queen Center 为共同出生点同点 NewNPC 125/126/134 + 2×139 探针
 *  (ai2=毁灭者头引用,ai3=∓1 侧位——原版第 7/8 参 ai2/ai3,曾误记 ai0)。
 *  调用方须已过 anyMechBossUp 门与 Mechdusa 种子门(remix+getGood)。 */
export function spawnMechQueenEnsemble(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Enemy | null {
  const queen = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 127, cx, cy);
  if (!queen) return null;
  // SpawnBoss :81469-81473：Type==127 且 mechQueen!=-1 → ai[3]=whoAmI(非零标记)；
  // 本仓 ai3 是旋冲方向缓存(±1)不冲突——mech 态旋转走 AngleLerp 不读 ai3,标记走 WeakSet
  markMechQueen(queen);
  queen.ai3 = queen.id;
  // :19743-19747 同点伴随(取 mechQueen 的 Center);探针 ai2=134 引用、ai3=∓1 侧位
  for (const id of [125, 126]) spawnVanillaEnemy(game, id, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  const head = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 134, queen.cx, queen.cy);
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const probe = spawnVanillaEnemy(game, 139, queen.cx, queen.cy);
    if (probe) { probe.ai2 = head ? head.id : -1; probe.ai3 = side; }
  }
  return queen;
}
```

```
  /** EoC 旋转追踪（cs:19975-20012 1:1）：向 target 以恒定 rate 线性步进（最短弧，
   *  环绕判定用原版 3.1415 字面量），步进后归一到 [0,6.283) 并在 |Δ|<rate 吸附带内
   *  直接对齐。rate=0（expert 且 hp<4%）时冻结朝向不步进。
   *  此前为 diff×min(1,rate*10) 指数逼近——快速档直接瞬移、慢速档永不到位，均偏离原版。 */
  private applyEocRotation(target: number, rate: number): void {
    let t = target;
    while (t < 0) t += Math.PI * 2;
    while (t >= Math.PI * 2) t -= Math.PI * 2;
    let a = this.visAngle;
    while (a < 0) a += Math.PI * 2;
    while (a >= Math.PI * 2) a -= Math.PI * 2;
    if (rate > 0) {
      if (a < t) {
        if (t - a > 3.1415) a -= rate; else a += rate;
      } else if (a > t) {
        if (a - t > 3.1415) a += rate; else a -= rate;
      }
      // 归一（cs:20001-20008 单步）+ 吸附带（cs:19997-20000/20009-20011 两次判定）
      if (a < 0) a += 6.283;
      else if (a > 6.283) a -= 6.283;
      if (a > t - rate && a < t + rate) a = t;
    }
    this.visAngle = a;
  }
```

```
    // pierce 语义（Projectile.cs:11903-11907/13152-13155 Damage_PVE）：
    //   0   = 近战/荆棘等非弹幕 → 命中设免疫 10t（原版 immune[owner] 默认档）；
    //   1   = 单发弹（maxPenetrate==1，flag3）→ **无视免疫且命中后不设帧**
    //         ——同 tick 多发（霰弹/多随从）全部生效,不再互挡（旧全局 iframes=8
    //         致 8 发四管霰弹只吃 1 发,DPS≈1/8,2026-08-13 修）；
    //   >1  = 穿透弹 → 设 10t;同弹重复命中由弹侧 hitSet 拦（原版 per-owner 槽单人
    //         近似;弹型覆写 1-8 表 :13032 未接,登记）
    // Critter 兼容：critters 桶的调用方按 Critter.hurt(game) 单参调用；
    // 原版小动物是 Enemy 进该桶，这里把 Game 对象重映射回 game 参、伤害取固定 5
    if (typeof dmg === 'object' && dmg !== null) {
      game = dmg as unknown as GameHooks;
      dmg = 5; kbx = 0; kby = 0;
    }
    const rawDmg = dmg; // 傀儡上报用原始值（下方 def 凘认算只做一次，双端一致）
    // 死亡单次门(用户报"多弹头武器打出双份僵尸碎块"):pierce=1 豁免免疫帧后,
    // 同 tick 多弹头(霰弹/食人鱼咬+随从)会全部进 hurt——首发致死后,后续弹头
    // 对"已死未出列"的敌怪二次触发死亡管线(碎块/掉落/音效翻倍)。原版由
    // Damage_PVE 的 npc.active 前置门(Projectile.cs:11869)天然防重;此处等价。
    if (this.dead) return false;
    if (pierce !== 1 && this.iframes > 0) return false; // 单发弹豁免免疫检查（flag3）
    // 旗 buff 加伤（Main.GetBannerBuffEffect → StrikeNPC 乘区,ItemID.cs:11-44 普通族:
    // 经典 ×1.5/专家 ×2;DD2 0.2 族不区分,登记）——统一入口=StrikeNPC 全通道语义
    if (this.vanillaId != null && (game as { bannerNpcActive?: (n: number) => boolean }).bannerNpcActive?.(this.vanillaId)) {
      const g = game as { world?: { flags?: Record<string, boolean> } };
      dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * (g.world?.flags?.hardMode ? 2 : 1.5)));
    }
    if (this.dontTakeDamage) return false; // 原版 NPC.dontTakeDamage（StrikeNPC 早退）
    this.justHitT = 2; // 原版 justHit 单帧旗标（AI_023 飞行武器/陆龟受击回态消费）
    // 训练假人（NPC 488，cs:83498 localAI 赋值）：immortal 永不死，
    // 受击只记摇晃强度+方向（渲染帧端消费），掉血由 dummyAI 回满

```
